# CbDD → Wikidata matches: people, 2026-10-20

Input: `missing/missing_people.csv`, all 1683 rows not yet in `to_match/` (`--skip-done`).
Batch: `people_2026-10-20_2.qs`, 689 commands. High: 689, medium: 130, no match: 864. Repairs: `people_2026-10-20_2_repair.qs`, 9 commands.

Candidates from `agentic-reconcile/scripts/people_candidates.py` (Wikidata full-text search over labels and aliases for several name variants, restricted to humans; items carrying the CbDD GND; the Wikidata link of the GND record via lobid), checked against the CbDD works with the lifetime rules of the skill. CbDD datings are often the building's whole history ("1385-1835/42"), so a work counts as fitting if any year of its dating fits. GND matches are high unless the identity itself is in doubt; name matches need a second independent hint (lifetime plus occupation, place, office or a CbDD alternative name).
